Every significant decision gets a row when it's made — this is the feedback loop that prevents drift.

| 105 | 2026-07-03 | Member-sheet QR (Damir question) — RECOMMENDATION: keep the QR OFF the default sheet. Flow: Accept → `setRequestAccepting(row)` latches the button to "Accepting…" (loading+disabled, aria-label synced) and disables Decline → `acceptContactRequest(listEl,state,req,opts)` removes the request and prepends a **handshaking chat** (excerpt `{type:'typing'}` "Establishing a quantum-secure handshake…", reusing the action-colored typing style + a gentle `[data-handshaking]` pulse, reduced-motion off) → the row is `aria-busy`, **un-openable** (tap routes to `onHandshakeBlocked`, never `onOpen`), **un-swipeable**, and shows only a **"Cancel handshake"** menu item → only the bridge signal `completeHandshake(...)` clears the flag, resets the excerpt, and bumps the timestamp so the just-secured contact surfaces on top. Timers live in the DEMO (mock bridge: 650ms ack → establishing → 2600ms complete), NOT the shell. **Recovery**: `failHandshake(...)` (symmetric to complete) removes a stranded chat — driven by the bridge failure/timeout signal (§9) OR the row's Cancel-handshake action — so a never-completing handshake is **never an un-removable trap** (the MAJOR the audit caught). Double-accept guarded (model `indexOf` + DOM disable); a late complete-after-cancel cannot resurrect a removed chat (triple guards). Desktop split-view: each pane (chat list · conversation · wallet · settings · apps) = its own native WebView; cross-pane coordination via C# (selectChat/selectTx), never a shared JS context; layout chrome (rail, dividers) may share a WebView but chat CONTENT never does. THE most important constraint in the project — supersedes any desktop "demo parity" ambition. |

HomePage: `onOverlayClosed` hook (Account close → setTheme/loadAvatar/shouldRefreshContacts; chat close → checkForRating); onChat unified (overlay tag "chat", wide col-1 / narrow full-span; dup-guard via getChatPage). | 234 | 2026-07-10 | 🟡 **RESUME/PRIVACY-LOCK BYPASS via the Cancel button (MAJOR — PRE-EXISTING, surfaced during the #230 lock scrutiny; NOT introduced by #227–#231; needs Damir on-device confirm + a design decision, per the #215 "verify on device" lesson).** Chain (all in the real tree): the RESUME lock is `new LockPage(true)` (App.xaml.cs:251) = `justConfirm` = **confirm mode**; `lock-shell.js lockSync` shows a **Cancel** button in confirm mode (`cancel.hidden = !confirm`, :123); `lock-page.js onCancel()` → `br.send('ixian:change')` (:55); `LockPage.onNavigating` for `ixian:change` under justConfirm fires `authSucceeded(this, false)` + closes the lock (`closeModalOverlay`/PopModal, :111-124); the resume lock's `authSucceeded` handler is `App.onUnlock`, which **ignores the bool** and unconditionally sets `isLockScreenActive=false` (App.xaml.cs:218-222). Net: tapping Cancel on the privacy lock dismisses it and unlocks the app WITHOUT the password — violates the ★ "lock must be un-dismissable" invariant. **Blast radius is limited to the resume lock:** the SettingsPage confirm-locks (delete wallet/account, lock-off) all check `e.Value`/`succeeded` and bail on false (`onDeleteWallet:299`, `onDeleteAccount:345`, `HandleAuthSucceeded:287`) — Cancel there safely aborts. Asymmetry proof: the BOOT lock is `new LockPage()` (unlock mode, no Cancel, only a "Use another wallet"→LaunchPage hatch) = correctly un-dismissable; only the resume lock's `true` opens the escape. **Why not a mechanical fix here:** `justConfirm` conflates two axes — (1) close-in-place-vs-rebuild-Home on success (resume wants close) and (2) show-a-Cancel-escape (resume must NOT). Guarding `App.onUnlock` on the bool alone is insufficient (Cancel still closes the lock → visible content with the latch stuck "locked"). The correct fix (resume lock in a confirm-close mode WITHOUT the Cancel escape, or a distinct lock mode) is a design/BE call. **✅ USER-CONFIRMED ON-DEVICE (Damir, 2026-07-10):** "when the app prompts lock + password you can bypass on Cancel; while actually REOPENING the app the lock screen doesn't have Cancel" — i.e. the resume/confirm lock's Cancel bypasses (confirmed) and the boot lock has no Cancel (confirmed un-dismissable). | 254 | 2026-07-11 | **★ SECURITY RULING — the Q12 `spixi.exdel` hint persists NO message text (shape `{del, t, kind}`).** The #253 flagged tradeoff, ruled by the Opus #46 loop (brief §non-negotiables demanded a keep-vs-drop verdict). **Finding that decided it:** the redesigned shells are loaded from a bare local path (`SPlatformUtils.getHtmlBaseUrl()` = `Config.spixiUserFolder + "/html/"` on Windows/iOS/Mac/Android → a `file://` document), and **mini-apps — third-party, publisher-supplied code — are loaded from `file://` + app_entry_point** (`MiniAppPage.xaml.cs:58`). In Chromium-based WebViews (WebView2, Android WebView) **all `file://` documents share ONE localStorage partition**; WKWebView likewise shares the default `WKWebsiteDataStore` across WebViews in the process unless explicitly configured. So the shells' localStorage is **plausibly readable by untrusted mini-app code** — SECURITY.md §1 / ★ #221 class. We therefore do not widen it by one line of COUNTERPART-authored message text: `writeExdelHint` emits `{del, t, kind}` only (chat.html), `excerptFromExdel` no longer reads `.text` (home.html), and a **text tail degrades to an empty excerpt line** (correct timestamp, self-heals on the next real push); non-text kinds (file/payment/app/call) still render their kind label from `window.SL`. Reversible: a one-field re-add IF BE confirms per-platform storage-partition isolation. **⚠ Escalated, NOT fixed here (goes to `docs/security-review-for-be-engineer.md`):** the SAME premise implicates already-shipped keys — `spixi.draft.<addr>` persists the user's OWN composed plaintext, and `spixi.exdel/likes/pins/mentions/app.declined` disclose contact addresses. ring rendered *under* the lock), and `LockPage.performUnlock`'s pop-the-top then popped the **ring** instead of the lock · **(2)** `hideSurface` popped the **TOP** modal (`PopModalAsync` has no by-page overload), so with a lock pushed above a modal ring (legacy page on top → resume → `App.OnResume`'s `CurrentPage` check cannot see a modal), a remote hang-up or the 45s ring timeout **popped the LOCK** — and `isLockScreenActive` stays latched ⇒ **no lock for the rest of the session** · **(3, re-review)** a lock that was still **STAGING** (`pushModalLoaded`, ~1.3s) was invisible to the first fix → the ring slipped in and ended up **unpoppable under the lock** · **(4)** answering a modal-fallback ring left the call with **NO UI and no hang-up** (`hideSurface`'s view work is dispatched even on the main thread, so `showBar`'s hide→ensure swap ran backwards) · **(5, FE)** the desktop bar rendered as a **clipped pill on an opaque band** (`callbar.css`'s #264 `:root[data-desktop]` rule, 0-3-0, out-specifies the shell's 0-2-1 — and the native stage IS the strip). **Landed:** `CallPage.lockUp()` (in-place · modal · boot/root · **staging**, via the new derived `SpixiContentPage.isLockStaging()`) → `ensureSurface` refuses and **re-arms `UIHelpers.refreshAppRequests`** · `App.OnResume` tears the surface down **before** staging the resume lock · `hideSurface` pops only when it is top-of-stack (fail-closed `Logging.error` tripwire), clears its statics **synchronously**, and re-asserts `broadcastCallState()` once the modal is really gone · `OnBackButtonPressed` → `isRingPresented()` (a modal that outlives its call stays dismissable) · the bar stage is **sized**, not margin-derived from an unmeasured `grid.Height` (which collapsed to a full-window input blocker) · avatar/contact snapshot hoisted **off** the UI thread (`imageToDataUri` does file I/O) · identity-scoped reveal timer · `setOverlayHost` tears down an orphaned call stage · `broadcastCallState` null-guards a mid-teardown `currentCallContact` · FE: `!ringEl.isConnected` latch reset, one-shot `hungUp` per session, unknown-`kind` no-op, desktop bar-mode strip override · **`smoke-test.mjs:3476` would have FAILED Damir's build** (stale `'empty_detail', 'wallet_sent']` regex vs the new `'…, 'call']` DEFAULT) → re-based, **+12 assertions** pinning every invariant · `Spixi.csproj` gained the `CallPage.xaml` MauiXaml entry (parity). **Behaviour Damir must sign off:** a call arriving while locked **rings audibly but shows no ring**; the ring/bar appears within one UI tick of the unlock (this IS the #258 accepted dial "no ring while locked" — now enforced rather than accidental). ★ **#221 re-verified:** the OUTBOUND wall is now structural (no broadcast exists at all; `setCallUi` reaches only CallPage's own WebView) and the **INBOUND** `acceptsCallPushes` gate was deliberately KEPT when the now-dead `getLivePages()` enumerator was deleted (`onAppAccept`/`onAppReject`/`hangUp` still refuse a mini-app; `MiniAppPage` still overrides it false). Money/wallet-send/frozen-bridge/#265 guards: untouched. **🟡 ON-DEVICE (the #215 gate):** ZIndex-vs-Children-order on WinUI is the ONLY thing keeping the lock above an in-place call stage and the call stage above a later overlay — if a platform falls back to insertion order, an overlay staged after a live ring **covers it** (re-order-Children fallback specified, not pre-implemented) · the lock↔call matrix in both orders · ring→bar flash · the desktop strip (art dial) · iOS notch/scrim. **🟡 Logged, not fixed:** `PopModalAsync` is pop-the-top **everywhere** in this tree (safe only because nothing stacks modals now — any future second modal re-opens the class → refactor-phase `popModal(page)` helper) · `OnDisappearing → Dispose()` is not `ModalStack`-guarded (unreachable today; widening it needs MAUI ordering semantics we cannot verify from the tree, #215) · the ring's first paint is tick-driven (≤1s; one line at the BE pass: `broadcastCallState()` at the end of `VoIPManager.onReceivedCall`). | Opus #46 loop (3 auditors + fixes + 1 fresh break-my-verdict re-reviewer) over `docs/opus-review-brief-quirks-final.md` §Batch Q4 | ✅ Q4 PASS → 🟡 **Damir: `git rm src/components/call-ui.js` → FULL build (`build-demo-bundle` → `build-shells` → `smoke-test`) → net10.0-windows (NOT Rebuild; NEW files `Spixi/Pages/Call/CallPage.xaml(.cs)`) → the #270 2-device F5 checklist + the lock↔call matrix → commit Q4 LAST** → then freeze → `git tag audit-baseline` → `docs/audit-refactor-plan.md` phase 1 |

| 281 | 2026-07-22 | **iOS-5/7/9 ROOT CAUSE (guarded-run log): OneSignal swizzling vs .NET registrar.** OneSignal moves the original `UNUserNotificationCenterDelegate` implementations to `onesignal`-prefixed selectors and invokes those; the .NET runtime resolves managed methods BY SELECTOR, the prefixed ones were unregistered → `ObjCRuntime.RuntimeException: Failed to lookup the required marshalling information` thrown at lookup, BEFORE the managed body — which is why the #280 guard alone could not catch it (guard kept: it covers body-level throws). Fix: `[Export]` the two prefixed selectors (`willPresentNotification` + `didReceiveNotificationResponse`) as forwarders to the real handlers, registering them with the managed runtime | The registrar maps selectors at build time; anything that renames selectors at runtime (swizzlers) needs the renamed selector exported too | 🟢 sim-verified 2026-07-22 (incoming call, foregrounded — no crash) |

| 289 | 2026-07-29 | **#288 F5 ROUND — tx-detail "Show amounts" rendered a BLANK card; root cause was NOT layout (shell-only → `build-shells`, NO bundle rebuild; fix audited, 0 BREAKS).** Damir F5: *"narrow window, hidden balance, open detail, click Show amounts → doesn't show amounts."* **Root cause:** `checkTransaction()` pushes `clearEntries` as its FIRST statement (`WalletSentPage:121`) and then **early-returns on an unchanged activity status** (`:129-133`) — no `addEntry`, no `setData`; `updateScreen()` re-polls at 1 Hz for as long as `!isConfirmedDisplayed`, which is only ever set for an `ActivityStatus.Final` tx (`:137`). So on ANY pending/unknown transaction the shell's entry buffer is empty a second after open while the painted card still shows the last good burst. Safe while `setData` was `render()`'s ONLY caller — #285's per-view reveal added a SECOND caller, so revealing rebuilt from `{}`: chrome intact, no money in it. **A CONFIRMED tx stops polling and reveals fine — that is the whole reason it looked narrow-only** (Damir happened to test the two widths on differently-confirmed transactions). The buffer can also be empty AT open, not only 1 s later: `onLoad`'s own `checkTransaction` takes the early return whenever the 1 Hz tick already ran a pass, which it can — `pageLoaded` is the readyState poll, NOT `ixian:onload`. **Fix: STAGING buffer** — `clearEntries`/`setReceivedMode`/`addEntry` write to `staging`, `setData` COMMITS into `model` and renders → `render()` is idempotent for every caller and the long-documented "buffer reset ONLY — never clears the rendered card" contract finally holds for re-renders too. Push order verified safe on every branch (`clearEntries :121` → `setReceivedMode :208` → `addEntry :187/:225` → `setData :236`; no path pushes `setData` without a preceding `clearEntries`, none pushes `setReceivedMode` first, none pushes `addEntry` after `setData`). **Proven with a jsdom harness against the BUILT shell: the pre-fix build fails 5 assertions exactly as reported, the post-fix build is 13/13** (harness was throwaway; the durable guard is a new smoke assertion pinning the staging contract). **Adversarial audit of the fix → 0 BREAKS**, diagnosis confirmed at file:line; landed from it: **R1** the reveal eye was offered during the fail-closed *unknown* window while the first `setHideBalance` push unconditionally clears `revealed` → a tap there was silently undone; the eye now waits for the flag (`canReveal()`) · **R10** the mask predicate was inline at two call sites → one `isMasked()` (two edits from disagreeing = either a masked card with no reveal, or an unmasked card that still offers one) · **R8** comment drift corrected, incl. one line that underpinned the C# safety argument (an UNCHANGED-value `setHideBalance` re-push **preserves** the reveal — that early return is exactly what makes #288's balance-toggle broadcast to open panes safe; do not "simplify" it away) · **audit correction:** the "a RECEIVED payment's `+` flipped to `-`" symptom written into the first fix comment is **NOT observable** (an empty entry renders no amount, so no sign is emitted) → recognise a recurrence by the **BLANK card**, not a wrong sign. **Caught on myself:** the R10 refactor broke my own #288 smoke assertion (it pinned the inline predicate) → re-pinned to the helper form before it could fail Damir's run — the #272/stale-regex class, twice in one session. **Pre-existing C# robustness surfaced by the audit, NOT fixed (BE, verify-first):** the `activity == null` path sets `confirmed = "error"` then dereferences `activity.timestamp` one line later (`:153-160`) → NRE that from `onLoad` propagates out of a `WebNavigating` handler and strands the boot spinner · `lastActivityStatus` is latched (`:133`) BEFORE `ctransaction = activity.transaction`, so a throw after the latch means the burst is never retried · `lastActivityStatus = 0` is a sentinel that collides with any `ActivityStatus` member valued 0 · non-Final terminal states poll forever. All need the IXICore enum/nullability to settle. 🟡 **Damir: local `smoke-test` + `pseudo-locale-smoke` → build net10.0-windows → F5: hidden balance → open a PENDING tx (not a confirmed one) → Show amounts → amount/name/fiat/fee all appear; leave it open a few seconds first, then reveal → still correct.** | ✅ fixed + audited · 🟡 F5 owed |

| 291 | **iOS-27 CRASH FIXED + device-F5 round on #290 (2026-07-29, same session; C# + components + shells → FULL build; UNCOMMITTED with #290).** **iOS-27 (the headline): the device build DIAGNOSED ITSELF** — `MT4174: Unable to locate the block to delegate conversion method for OneSignalWillPresentNotification(...)'s parameter #3`, and the same for the response handler. #281's hand-written `[Export]`s of OneSignal's prefixed selectors carry an `Action<UNNotificationPresentationOptions>` **block** parameter with no `[BlockProxy]`, so no trampoline is generated and the runtime aborts at selector lookup — the exact `.ips` signature #281 was written to fix. Debug's DYNAMIC registrar downgrades it to a warning, which is precisely why the simulator "verified" it twice. **This also closes the B3 gate without the separate Release build.** FIX: the custom `UNUserNotificationCenterDelegate` is **DELETED** rather than patched — every behaviour it held already exists in OneSignal's own managed events (`Clicked` → the `fa` deep link, wired since forever; `WillDisplay` → foreground display, which now also honours the `alert` flag the override read). Owning the delegate was itself the bug: `class_addMethod` FAILS when the class already defines the selector, so our class was what broke OneSignal's swizzle — deleting it means OneSignal installs cleanly, which **also closes B1** (delegate starvation) **and B2** (two live owners of a push tap with divergent navigation). Three runsheet rows retired by one deletion. ⚠ #215: Damir must confirm on device that a foreground push still displays and a tap still deep-links. **Damir F5 round on #290:** iOS-36 group Address card + QR suppressed for ALL groups (a group has no wallet address — the value is a session id and the QR resolved to nothing; the card was previously hidden only for BLIND groups, wrong premise) · iOS-37 the #290 group Message action never rendered — `contact_details` forces `context:'chat'` for groups so the title reads "Group info", and the component gated Message on `context === 'contact'`; now it renders whenever `onMessage` is supplied, and the shell supplies it unless entered from inside the chat · iOS-38 the "Select at least 2 people" hint collapsed its line box at the 2nd selection and JUMPED the contact list mid-tap → the line stays mounted and swaps to "{n} selected" (same height, no reflow, `role=status` announces it) · iOS-39 the Request-from-contact strip was loose rows capped at 5, so reaching anyone else REQUIRED typing → card surface + scrolling box, cap raised to 50 as a DOM guard only. **Confirmed working on device:** iOS-14 · iOS-21 · iOS-23 (mechanism) · iOS-26 · iOS-28 · iOS-30 · iOS-33 · iOS-35 pinch-to-text · the money fence. **Logged OPEN, not built:** iOS-40 Account-tab lag (Account is NOT an in-page tab — it pushes a separate ~1.6MB SettingsPage WebView that cold-boots every open; #290's deferral removed the bridge stall but not the WebView start, so the real fix is keeping that WebView warm — its own unit) · iOS-41 "Select text" affordance (Damir call; needs a `cursor-text` export) · **iOS-42 the Terms/Privacy copy is PLACEHOLDER** (`TERMS_DEFAULT`/`PRIVACY_DEFAULT`, #169 summaries — the real agreed documents were never supplied; drop-in via `strings.termsBody`/`privacyBody`, and note ONBOARDING shows the same placeholders) · iOS-43 clipped "Launch ..." label. | 292 | **iOS-31/8/11 unread badge — THREE bugs, all in-tree (2026-07-29; C#-only).** Device F5 kept the badge alive after accept→open→back. (a) `SingleChatPage:1270` pushed `friend.getUnreadMessageCount()` — a re-derivation from message flags, and a `requestAdd` is never markable-read — instead of the literal `0` it had just written to metaData, so the "zeroing" push re-asserted the badge. (b) `contactStatusCacheItem` is a **STRUCT**: `HomePage:2484` copied it out of the list, mutated the copy, never wrote back → an existing cache entry never updated and `updateContactStatus` kept pushing the ORIGINAL values. | 294 | **iOS-29 keyboard — ATTEMPTED AND REVERTED; third failure (2026-07-29).** Device measurement finally settled the premise questions: `[innerHeight, vv.height, vv.offsetTop] = [852, 544, 0]` → the #283 premise HOLDS (delta 308 >> 60) and the `visualViewport` **resize event DOES fire** (852 → 544 logged). The failure is downstream of both: `document.body.style.height` read back `""` (competing `html, body { height: 100% }`), and forcing it with `setProperty(…, 'important')` DID shrink body and lift the composer **in the console** — ~40px high, because WebKit keeps reporting `env(safe-area-inset-bottom)` at full value with the keyboard up. Shipped, though, the same change **did not work AND introduced a visible artifact** (on composer focus the topbar slides up and a second topbar animates in below). Conclusion: resizing `body` while WKWebView runs its own focus pan is the wrong lever, console-verified or not. **Reverted to the pre-#294 shape; nothing shipped.** Next attempt must CHANGE THE LEVER (position the chat column against `visualViewport`, or handle it natively on keyboard notifications) — full measurement set preserved in the iOS-29 findings row so it is never re-derived. | ⛔ reverted · 🟡 open |

| 298 | **★ CHAT TRANSPORT — the highest-leverage C# change in the cutover (2026-07-30) → `docs/chat-transport-spec.md`, a standalone BE work order.** Damir asked for scroll-triggered history instead of a button. Investigation showed his three asks (scroll history · faster chat entry · drop the anti-flicker delay) are **one root cause**. (a) **There is no prepend.** `onLoadMore` grows a window and re-flushes: `clearMessages` + re-add of the ENTIRE grown window (`:371-376` → `loadMessages` :1234-1300). Scroll-triggered history on that transport is WORSE than a button — page 4 tears down ~400 rows while the user is mid-scroll. (b) **Every message is its own WebView round trip**: `insertMessage` → `sendUiCommand` → `evaluateJavascript`, each with its OWN `MainThread.BeginInvokeOnMainThread` + `EvaluateJavaScriptAsync` (`SpixiContentPage.cs:150-164`), plus an `updateReactions` push per message (:1288) — opening a chat is **100+ marshals**. That is the cost of chat entry. (c) the 250 ms burst-safety timer exists only because there is **no end-of-batch signal** after a loadmore (`onChatScreenLoaded` fires only from `onLoad`, :588). **Four-part fix, all small: B1** `addMessages(json, position)` — one crossing per batch instead of one per message · **B2** `onLoadMore` pushes only the older SLICE with `position:"prepend"`, no `clearMessages` (O(n) instead of O(n²)) · **B3** `messagesDone()` → the shell DELETES its burst gate and the 250 ms timer outright, flicker fixed by construction not by delay · **B4** `Config.messagesToLoad` 100→~30. Then `lazy-history.js` (written, unwired since it was built) finally has a matching transport, and the A1 pill is swapped for a scroll sentinel (~60% of the FE work carries over). **UNVERIFIED, needs BE:** whether Ixian-Core's `getMessages(channel,count)` can return a slice or only a last-N (C# can compute the slice locally either way — `loadMessages` already does with `skip_messages` :1253-1258). No wallet/key/path risk — reviewed against the "C# touches no risky parts" rule; this is a pure transport change, NOT part of the security bundle. | 🟡 BE work order written, not started |

| #338 | 2026-08-13 | **W2 CASE A REVERTED AFTER AUDIT — the desktop-rail C# fix was NET-NEGATIVE and is NOT in the tree. MULTI-USER APP LAUNCH ON THE NEW PICKER (single-target) KEPT.** Case A was built under Damir's approved "write it, flag it clearly" exception, then the #46 adversarial pass found it breaks money flows on a wide window and it was reverted to pristine in the same session. **Why it was wrong:** `popPageAsync()` means two different things — in the push world it pops the TOP of the stack, in the overlay world it is identity-aware (`SpixiContentPage` ~2067-2080, `overlayStack.Find(o => o.target == this)`) and closes the CALLER'S own overlay. So Wallet→Send→pick recipient→tap a contact destroyed the Send page while leaving the recipient list up (payment uncompletable); `WalletSentPage.onDismiss` indexed `NavigationStack[Count-2]` on a detached proxy and threw on the UI thread; QR→add-contact left the form open and could pop HomePage itself where HomePage is not the nav root. The `legacyStageTimeoutMs = 400` failsafe rested on a FALSE premise: `wallet_request` / `wallet_recipient` / `wallet_contact_request` DO emit `ixian:onload` (their `<body onload="onload()">` resolves to `js/spixi.js:88`), so 400 ms was a race against WebView2 boot, not a safety net. And the rail would have been visible-but-inert anyway: the `ixian:tab:` sweep closes only settings/contact-details/formpane/txdetail overlays, so the newly-staged wallet pages were in no sweep list. **W2 case A therefore remains OPEN** — the rail is still covered by legacy full-window pushes on desktop; a correct fix has to make each converted page's pop path overlay-aware and extend the tab sweep FIRST. Case B (takeover insets) + `--layout-rail-width` were already shipped in the #337 tree and are untouched; the drift guard between `railWidthDip` and `--layout-rail-width` was kept (it still describes the #245 Account peer-pane). **Multi-user app launch (kept, additive C# only):** the apps tab + the ⋮ invite no longer emit `ixian:startAppMulti` (whose C# answer was the legacy `WalletRecipientPage` — Damir's complaint); they open the in-shell picker (purpose 'app') and confirm with the new `ixian:startappwith:<appId>:|<addr>` verb → `HomePage.onStartAppWith`. **SINGLE-target**, matching what the legacy selector actually did: `MiniAppPage` still has `// TODO randomize session id and add support for more users` (sessionId = sha3(appId)), `sendNetworkData` relays only to the single `friendOrGroup` and `hasUser` rejects everyone else — so a >1 fan-out would invite people to a session the host never talks to and whose data it drops. Picker is `role=radio`, second pick replaces the first, bridge caps at 1; group creation untouched (checkbox, min 2). AppDetailsPage has no roster → closes itself and calls `HomePage.pickAppTargets(appId)`. Picker check-circle step-up (`--icon-neutral-03`) was ALREADY in the tree; measured 4.30:1 light / 5.69:1 dark. | 🟡 **C# UNVERIFIED — F5 FIRST** (only the additive `ixian:startappwith:` path remains: multi-user app launch from the apps tab AND from app details) |
| #339 | 2026-08-13 | **W9 — WINDOWS F5 BATCH (Damir, 4 items).** **① Wallet "Request an amount" → MULTI-SELECT + ONE CTA** (verbatim: *"when I sent a request to someone I still remain in the same screen with input active and I can add more … perhaps we can have a multiselect as for group creation and then 1 SEND REQUEST button that then confirms it was sent, and we return to wallet screen"*). Rows adopt the group-creation grammar 1:1 (`role=checkbox` + `aria-checked` + the `--icon-neutral-03` check circle, contacts-shell `pickerRow`); the rule/count line is the `c-contacts__minhint` pattern (same element, text swapped, never hidden — hiding it collapses its box and jumps the list under a finger). **CTA label = `Request {a} IXI ({n})`** ("Send request" while inert): on a money surface the button is the last thing the eye is on, so it restates the amount the user typed AND the recipient count at the moment of commitment; the parenthetical keeps it one line in every locale and the full sentence lives in `aria-label`. **The per-row send arrow, `state.latch`, `[data-acted]`, the ✓ morph and the `[data-needs-amount]` arrow gate are DELETED** — selecting is not sending, so nothing on a row needs gating; the amount rule moved onto the CTA. **#72④ double-fire protection survives, on the CTA** (`state.sending` latches the loop). **The bridge verb stays per-contact** (`ixian:sendrequest:<addr>:<amount>` looped once per pick — no batch verb was invented, the protocol is frozen); `onSendRequest` returning `false`/throwing marks THAT recipient as not sent. **Partial failure never navigates:** the sent ones untick, the failures stay ticked, an error-tone result line says so, and the CTA count follows down — "try again" retries exactly the remainder, never a duplicate to someone who already got one. All-clear → `onRequestsSent` → shell toasts + closes the takeover (return to Wallet). Collapsing the section clears amount AND selection (the #303 state-honesty rule extended). #303 untouched: the QR stays constant `address:ixi`. **② `chat-info` room Message action** (*"Group info if I delete chat I cant reactivate it — theres no Send message in group details"*): the rule is now kind-agnostic (`kind 'group' \| 'bot'` + supplied `onMessage` → a lone Message action; no `onMessage` → NO row at all, so the in-chat takeover stays free of it). iOS-26 had given GROUPS this action; #249 then moved BOT/channel surfaces onto the same screen and the `kind === 'group'` test did not follow — a channel reached from the directory still dead-ended. Empty action rows are no longer appended. **③ App-details multi-user launch** (*"the selection from app details closes the app details pane and returns to chat"*): `AppDetailsPage.onStartAppMulti` now HANDS OFF FIRST and tears itself down SECOND — **CORRECTED BY #340 (C-MINOR-6): there was no order to reverse.** At `40f74cf4` `onStartAppMulti` contained NO `popPageAsync()` at all (it pushed `WalletRecipientPage`); the pop lived in `HandlePickAppMultiUserSucceeded`, i.e. it ran AFTER the pick. The symptom Damir saw is that closing this overlay (formpane, column 1) drops the detail column back to the conversation — which still happens, because we still pop. What actually saves the flow is `home.pickAppTargets` parking the shell on APPS first. The ordering is kept deliberately (popping first arms closeOverlay's teardown before the pick request is issued) but it is NOT the load-bearing part — the shell-side park is. Do not "simplify" the shell side away (`popPageAsync()` is not a plain call — for an overlay-mode page it queues a main-thread teardown, +250 ms slide on iOS), and `home.pickAppTargets` parks the shell on APPS before opening the picker, so neither the launch nor the abandon path can end on the chats list. **④ Desktop drag-to-extend in `chat-select`**: inside selection mode only, mouse only, anchor→pointer range fill with the pre-drag baseline preserved outside the range, text selection suppressed for the gesture only. A drag OUTSIDE selection mode still selects TEXT. | 🟡 ①②④ shell-side, shipped · ③ **C# UNVERIFIED — F5 FIRST** (`AppDetailsPage.onStartAppMulti` ordering only) |
| #340 | 2026-08-13 | **THE OWED #46 AUDIT over the #339/#338 C# RAN → 3 ROUNDS → CLEAN** (Cowork/cloud session; 3 disjoint read-only auditors → fixes → 2 fresh break-my-verdict reviewers → r2 fix → r3 confirming reviewer). **STATE CORRECTION FIRST: the batch was NOT uncommitted.** `docs/handoff-2026-08-16.md` said "everything on Damir's disk, UNCOMMITTED, HEAD still `40f74cf4`" — it is committed as **`0ebf5d0b`** and pushed to `origin/redesign/frontend`. The audit therefore ran over `40f74cf4..HEAD` (5 files, +257/−5), not a working tree. **COMPILER-VERIFIED FOR THE FIRST TIME (partially):** .NET 8 SDK installed in the cloud container and Roslyn `csc` run over all 5 files — **zero CS1xxx**, i.e. they parse. A full MAUI compile is impossible offline (no workloads, nuget.org blocked), so TYPE/overload/nullability checking is still owed to Damir's F5. **4 MAJORs FOUND + FIXED.** **(1) A-MAJOR-1/2 — the `sendUiCommand` data-URI fast path was gated on the VALUE, not the RECEIVER, and "round-trip identical" silently assumed every receiver runs `native.js`. Two do not.** The 8 remaining legacy `Raw/html` pages decode with `js/spixi.js`'s **unguarded `atob`**: a peer-chosen nickname of `data:;base64,x` passes the whitelist, is emitted verbatim, and `atob` THROWS on the `:` — dropping the whole push. `wallet_contact_request.setData` is that page's only writer, so **a hostile requester blanks the payment-confirm screen** (recipient, amount and date all empty) while `ixian:send` still works. Deterministic, not racy. Second receiver: **MiniAppPage**, whose `SpixiAppSdk.onNetworkData` carries PEER BYTES to a decoder that ships inside third-party app packages and can never be regenerated — an ordinary `data:image/png;base64,…` payload between two users of a whiteboard app would have started throwing. FIX: new `SpixiContentPage.supportsRawDataUriArgs` (`loadedHtmlFileName != null && !hasLegacyPageChrome(…)`), **fails closed** — MiniAppPage never calls `loadPage` so the field is null there, and a push before load is encoded (pre-#339 behaviour). Whitelist kept as the SECOND gate. Perf win verified still live on `index.html` (`loadedHtmlFileName` is assigned synchronously in the HomePage ctor, long before any `addApp`). Legacy-page list proved byte-identical to the `grep -L viewport-fit=cover` detector AND to `grep -l js/spixi.js`. **(2) B-MAJOR-1 — `closeSublevelOverlays()` was blind to a STAGING sublevel.** `pushPageLoaded` is load-then-present, so for the whole boot window (up to the 4 s timeout) the page is in `activePreload`, NOT `overlayStack`, with its stage **already parented to HomePage's grid** — and nothing cancels it when its opener dies. That window is exactly when the user leaves: the screen deliberately shows the unchanged Account hub while the pane loads, so "I clicked and nothing happened" → tap a rail tab → Account closes → **the password pane then presents itself over the NEXT tab with a live WebView**, escapable only by its own back arrow (no hardware back on WinUI). Same behaviour that produced the original W7 report. FIX: sweep covers `getStagingPage()` too (`popPageAsync()` → `abandoned` + `cancelPreload`, the tested path); `HomePage.requestSettingsOverlayExit`'s DIRECT-close `else` branch now sweeps before `removePage` (an OS theme flip clears `pageLoaded` via `reloadAllPages`, so that branch is live); method `private` → `internal`. **r2 (both reviewers, independently): the type test was too narrow** — the Account stages THREE sublevels the same way, so `is EncryptionPassword` became `|| BackupPage || DownloadsPage`. Backup/Downloads are cap-gated to non-pane mode so desktop never fires them, but **on mobile they are ordinary overlays with the identical strand**. List kept EXPLICIT: `pushModalLoaded` shares `activePreload`, so a `!= null` sweep would cancel the resume LOCK. **(3) C-MAJOR-1 — the `appsPushedToShell` perf latch removed the per-tab-entry SELF-HEAL, turning any lost or interleaved apps push from transient into permanent-for-the-session.** Proven route: `loadApps` runs on TWO threads with no marshalling — the UI thread on tab3 entry and `Node.updateUILoop`'s thread-pool tick via `updateScreen` (`Node.cs:389-407` → `OnUpdateUI` → `updateScreen:2233`). Install an app, tap Apps within the next second: both pass the gate, and the tick's `clearApps` lands between the tap's `addApp` calls — the shell drops rows it already had, and the latch then made that short list STICK. FIX: `lock (appsPushLock)` (NEW private lock, deliberately not `refreshLock`, to stay out of the chats/contacts lock order — order proved uniformly `appsPushLock → appList`, no inversion, no blocking main-thread marshal inside); latch set **after** the loop as `= pageLoaded` not `= true` before it (`sendMessage` queues while unloaded and `Dispose()` drops that queue); latch cleared on the DOCUMENT dying (`reload()` AND `reloadShell()`, which calls `base.reload()` directly and bypasses the override) as well as in `onLoaded()` — `ixian:onload` is the known-racy WinUI handshake the #337 belt exists for, and **apps was the one surface with no other recovery**; field `volatile`. **(4) C-MINOR-3 — `onStartAppWith` invited people to apps that do not exist.** `MiniApp.id` comes verbatim out of a downloaded `appinfo.spixi` with NO charset validation, so an id containing the `":|"` delimiter mis-splits; worst case a real address lands in `parts[0]` and we **send a network app-invite plus a chat card for a nonexistent app**, then open a blank WebView (`getAppEntryPoint` → null → `"file://"`). FIX: `getApp(appId) == null` → return, BEFORE any invite. **MINORs:** `rehomeOverlay` now moves `op.stageMargin` with the real margin (forward guard — r1 correctly noted the sets are disjoint today, comment now says so); the AppDetailsPage rationale comment was factually FALSE and is corrected (see the #339 correction above). **CRLF SMOKE BRITTLENESS KILLED** (the handoff's second task): one aliased import + a 4-line normalize-on-read wrapper in `scripts/smoke-test.mjs`. Verified on a full CRLF twin, both directions — with the shim the twin reports the same **1435/same-4** as LF; with it reverted the twin fails 8, a strict superset containing the 3 Damir saw (`#148③`, `#309b`, `#315`) plus 5 the handoff had not found (06d payment-pane, 06d⓫ app-details CTA, 06d⓬ call dialog, 2 BUG-1 chat-list). Nothing passes without the shim and fails with it, so it cannot mask a regression; no assertion in the file has byte semantics. **+14 mutation-honest pins, ALL individually mutation-verified** (r2 killed one that was mutation-DEAD — it grepped a COMMENT in `SpixiContentPage.cs` instead of the invariant in `MiniAppPage.xaml.cs`; r3 anchored the widened type list on its closing paren so an OVER-broad list also fails, and made the MiniAppPage pin comment-blind). smoke **BASELINE OK 1435 / same 4** (#136 · #149③ · M5 · B3), cloud full run. Built shells confirmed in sync with `src/` (rebuild → zero diff), so the #258/#288 stale-shell class does not apply here. **LOGGED, NOT PATCHED (deliberate):** `MiniApp.id` is unvalidated at INSTALL, and `HomePage.onNavigating` dispatches on `Contains()` for 8 earlier verbs — a hostile id embedding `ixian:tab:` wins the branch race before any handler guard can run (real fix = charset-validate at install; BE brief). `hasLegacyPageChrome` now carries TWO unrelated invariants (iOS insets + data-URI transport) — they move together today, a separate `hasLegacyBridgeDecoder` list would make it explicit. `SettingsPage:267` still stages Backup with `paneMode ? 1 : -1` (moot now the sweep is widened, but the geometry is still wrong if the cap push is ever lost). Two grammars: rows tint only, controls tint + `scale(0.97)`; a list row that scales reads as a mistake, and both iOS and Android tint rows without moving them. 90 ms in / 120 ms out, reduced-motion escape, passive listeners so scrolling stays on the compositor. Chase it later with a content-hashed filename AND a measurement. Also NOT done: a virtual `https://appassets…` origin. Separate decision, separate batch. **THE FLOOR THAT REMAINS:** ~185 ms of JS compile and execute survives this change, because the same bytes still arrive and still run. Now: `exiting` gates PAINTING and self-heals after 2500 ms; a separate `exitSent` gates outbound verbs. Auto-save commits nickname, avatar and app-lock as they change, with a toast. **The security lens changed the design twice.** Saving the nickname broadcasts to every contact, so a typing debounce was never an option — the component already commits on Enter/blur, so the broadcast count is unchanged. And app-lock is persisted from C#'s CONFIRMATION push, not from the toggle: `ixian:lock:off` opens an auth modal and only resolves later, so saving at toggle time would persist the PRE-AUTH value and clear the dirty flag with it, leaving the lock permanently on. The ON path now confirms too (one C# line), because auto-save removes the Save control and ON had no other trigger. **A9 — the landscape Account pane, and the premise was false.** The peer-pane inset exists to leave the home nav RAIL visible, and the settings shell hides its own tab bar because that rail is supposed to be the exit. But the rail is drawn by the home WebView behind `:root[data-desktop]`, a UA sniff that NEVER fires on Android. The only test was a 700-DIP width check, and a landscape phone is 700-950 DIP wide. The gate is now `DeviceInfo.Platform` (WinUI or MacCatalyst) — the review rejected `DeviceInfo.Idiom`, which is posture-dependent (a Surface with its keyboard detached reports Tablet). ONE boolean drives the branch AND the park guard, so they cannot disagree. **W8 — tip in bot groups, and it needed C#.** The FE option was OFF because C# hard-blocked every bot; the bot branch that resolves the recipient was DEAD code. Blind chats stay blocked, and the test had to MOVE to say so: a bot is chat_type 3 regardless of `hideParticipantAddresses`, so a blind BOT would have fallen through a `type == Group` test. `setChatMode` gained an additive 7th argument for blindness, because the type cannot carry it. **A5 / W4b — the centre-out fill.** A one-colour gradient sized `0% → 100% 100%` from a centred origin. A pseudo-element state layer was rejected: the pressed tokens are OPAQUE, so an overlay would cover the row's own text. **W4a** moved the tx row's selected colour onto the chat-row tokens, in the component, beside the chat row's identical rule. **W1 — measured, not guessed.** The chat spinner is invisible BY DESIGN (`webView.Opacity = 0` until after `loadMessages`); chat info has neither line, which is the whole asymmetry. And the open waits on DATA ARRIVAL, not the settle timer — the push lands behind the last message on a healthy open. What IS real: `onChatScreenLoaded` was pushed from exactly ONE place, so the bot channel switch ALWAYS paid the full 250 ms fallback. One line fixes it. No skeleton was built — #294. **THE #46 LOOP RAN TWICE AND EARNED IT.** Three disjoint auditors found 12 MAJOR, several mine. A fresh break-my-verdict reviewer then found 8 MORE in the FIXES, including a CRITICAL: `Node.prepareTransactionFrom` RETURNS NULL on insufficient funds, every line below dereferenced it, and the throw escapes `onNavigating` before `e.Cancel` — so an over-balance tip navigated the WebView to the raw `ixian:` URL and destroyed the conversation. The reviewer also proved my first blind-bot fix made things WORSE: gating only the member sheet pushed the sender label into `message-bubble`'s no-sheet branch, which puts the full address in `title`, in `aria-label`, and ONE TAP from the clipboard. It also caught `goToWelcome` disposing the parked overlay BEFORE the pop that parks it — a guaranteed no-op that left the WIPED account warm and re-presentable — and a co-located `:active` rule painting the same colour flat, which made the whole centre-out fill invisible on every Chromium engine. **smoke BASELINE OK 1599 / the same 4 pre-existers**, +59 assertions. Three of my own pins were DEAD on first write: `/100%/` is satisfied by the REST value `0% 100%`, so the pin claiming the sweep completes passed with the size flip DELETED; and the fill was on the wrong element for `.c-app-item` while a pin checked only that a transition STRING mentioned `background-size`. Every A5 pin now compares the size EXACTLY, probes all seven row families, and a source-level sweep catches a `background` shorthand in any state — the one thing jsdom cannot see, because it never matches `:hover` or `:active` on a probe node. | ⚠️ **BLOCKED ON THE CORE GATE.** Ixian-Core moved `0.9.8j` → `0.9.8k` and the app has not been rebuilt. 🟡 Damir, IN THIS ORDER: (1) wipe `obj/bin`, rebuild, F5 chat send + receive on the CLEAN tree — that is the Core-only test, and nothing of mine is on your disk until it passes; (2) then apply the tarball, `build-demo-bundle` → `build-shells` → `smoke-test` → build → F5. **NOT device-tested.** **DAMIR'S CALL (asked and answered in-session): route to welcome as instructed.** With it, `LaunchRestorePage` gained the `getWalletList().Count > 0` guard `LaunchCreatePage` already had — delete-account keeps the wallet, so a live wallet can now sit behind onboarding for the first time, and Restore was the one door that would have run straight over it. ⚠ Known and accepted: Create still refuses in that state until a restart, and `onboardingComplete` is untouched, so a cold start boots back into the app. **★ THE #46 LOOP RAN FIVE ROUNDS, and stopped only when a round found no product defect.** R1: 3 disjoint auditors → 12 MAJOR. R2: a fresh reviewer → 8 more IN THE FIXES, one CRITICAL (`prepareTransactionFrom` returns NULL on insufficient funds and every line below dereferenced it — an over-balance tip threw, the throw escaped `onNavigating` before `e.Cancel`, and the WebView navigated to the raw `ixian:` URL and destroyed the conversation). What the pass found became `docs/f5-findings-2026-08-15.md` — 12 defects and 9 ideas, each with file:line evidence, an owner and an effort. **D-10 — THE MONEY BUG, AND THE ONE THAT JUSTIFIES THE BATCH.** The tip sheet morphed to a green "✓ Tipped" the instant the verb was emitted, while a native Insufficient Balance alert sat on top of it saying the opposite. `ctrl.done()` means "the verb left", not "the money moved" — the same disease as D-4 (kick), and worse on a money surface. Fix B was built, not A: a `setTipResult` push, the precedent being `setEncPassResult` from #341. It carries the status, the failure body and **the message id**, because without correlation a late answer for tip A resolves a sheet the user has since opened on message B. It is **capability-gated** (`setCaps "tipResult"`) — without the gate a new shell on an old exe freezes 12 s after a SUCCESSFUL tip and then claims it may have failed, and an old shell on a new exe shows no error at all; both are worse than the bug. It has a **12 s backstop timer**, because the sheet disables light-dismiss and Esc while money is in flight, so an answer that never arrives is a frozen screen. And success **restates the amount** in a toast: removing the native alert also removed the only place that said what was paid, since the reaction pill carries a txid, not a value. **D-11 RESOLVED BY DELETION.** The alert asked "Tip <group name>?" instead of naming the member. A nick ladder was written to fix it — and the audit proved the whole ladder was DEAD CODE, because both branches answer through `sendTipResult`, which takes no title, so `modal_title` was assigned and never read. C# emits no warning for that. The complaint is fixed by D-10 removing the alert; the name the user sees is the sheet's own header, which the shell already resolves correctly. **I-8 — DAMIR'S DIAL, AND THE AUDIT CAUGHT ME MATCHING A NAME INSTEAD OF A LOOK.** He asked for the pressed colour off, the SELECTED colour in, and slower. My first cut used `--surface-interactive-selected` because the token is NAMED "selected" — that token was rejected on this exact surface in 2026-07 at 1.027:1 in dark and DARKER than the canvas. `--surface-action-tonal-default` (1.413 light / 1.260 dark) is what a selected row actually paints. Open runs at `--duration-300`. **I-5** — every screen title is `neutral-01`; only the Chats logotype keeps the accent, and the accent came OUT of the root-variant rule so nothing else inherits it. **D-9② — a fatal start error can announce itself.** Damir's delete-account left the app on a black screen, and his VS output showed the error PATH throwing: `displaySpixiAlert` hosts on `Application.Current.MainPage` while MainPage is still being assigned. `safeFatalAlert` logs FIRST, then attempts the alert inside a try/catch. **THE #46 LOOP RAN AGAIN.** Two disjoint auditors → 3 HIGH. A fresh break-my-verdict reviewer then found that `Crypto.stringToHash` sat OUTSIDE the very try/catch its own comment named, and that **all four new pins were defeatable** — worst, M3: delete the shell's `setCaps` HANDLER and the capability never lands, 100% of tips take the `ctrl.done()` fallback, the original D-10 money bug returns, and the suite stays fully green.
